##### SOG (Support of Government) Setup #####

# The values are calulated based on government parties, political stability and government legitimacy 
# If it ever goes south, the values are logged in logs/game.log so you can see why the values were calculated the way they were, but you can ping @Syrexx
# This shorter version is thanks to Warleader

# The next scripted effects should be executed on coalition changes
RAD_SOC_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
 
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 0-40% (-10%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 41-50% (-3%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 56-60% (+1%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 61-65% (+3%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 66-70% (+5%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.81 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 71-80% (+6%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 81-100% (+7%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
 
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): stability -5..-3 (-10%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): stability -2..-1 (-3%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): stability 0 (-1%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): stability +1 (+2%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): stability +2 (+4%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): stability +3 (+6%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
}
RAD_SOC_modifiers_opp_neutral = { #  this block would go in scripted effects
 
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.15 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 0-40% (-15%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 41-50% (-5%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 51-60% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 61-65% (+1%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.04 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 66-70% (+2%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.81 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.06 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 71-80% (+4%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): legitimacy 81-100% (+6%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
 
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.15 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): stability -5..-3 (-15%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): stability -2..-1 (-5%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): stability 0 (-2%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): stability +1 (+1%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): stability +2 (+3%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "RadSoc(opp/neu): stability +3 (+6%) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
}
setup_rad_soc_sog = {
	# RadSoc
	set_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
		log = "RadSoc is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: RadSoc [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# RadSoc itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_radical_socialism compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "RadSoc has the most support in the coalition [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 65 max = 70 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Leader is Social Egalitarianism (close to RadSoc) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 55 max = 60 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Leader is Social Liberalism [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:radical_democracy compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 6 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Leader is Peripheral/Radical Democracy [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {	# A close ideology is present but isn't the largest party
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 6 }
					}
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the coalition but none lead [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = {
				set_rad_soc_to_opposition = yes	
				log = "RadSoc moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			RAD_SOC_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for RadSoc [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 } }
		log = "RadSoc is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:ideology_radical_socialism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "RadSoc is largest besides the ruling party [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 035 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Egalitarianism (close to RadSoc) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to RadSoc) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
					}
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = {
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			RAD_SOC_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for RadSoc [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 } }
		log = "RadSoc is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:ideology_radical_socialism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "RadSoc is largest besides the ruling party [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Egalitarianism (close to RadSoc) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals }
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to RadSoc) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
					}
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 5 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = {
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 5 max = 10 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No one close to RadSoc in the government [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			RAD_SOC_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = rad_soc_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for RadSoc [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "RadSoc is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

SOC_LIB_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-10% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-3% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+8% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+10% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-10% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-3% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
SOC_LIB_modifiers_opp_neutral = { #  this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-13% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-5% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.07 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+7% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+8% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-13% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-5% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-2% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+8% loyalty) (soc_lib_approval_of_government : [?soc_lib_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
setup_soc_lib_sog = {
	# Soclib
	set_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }
		log = "Soclib is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Soclib [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Soclib itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Soclib has the most support in the coalition [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 65 max = 70 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Egalitarianism (close to Soclib) [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:centrism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 50 max = 60 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Conservative Liberalism (close to Soclib) [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 } 
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 } 
					}
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_soc_lib_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "RadSoc moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			SOC_LIB_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Soclib [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 2 } }
		log = "Soclib is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Soclib is largest besides the ruling party [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Egalitarianism (close to Soclib) [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:centrism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Conservative Liberalism (close to Soclib) [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			SOC_LIB_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Soclib [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 2 } }
		log = "Soclib is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Soclib is largest besides the ruling party [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Egalitarianism (close to Soclib) [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:centrism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Conservative Liberalism (close to Soclib) [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			SOC_LIB_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = soc_lib_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Soclib [?soc_lib_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Soclib is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

SOC_EGA_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-10% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-3% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+8% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+10% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-10% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-3% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (soc_ega_approval_of_government : [?soc_ega_approval_of_government])"
	}

	# Socega
	set_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 3 } }
		log = "Socega is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Socega [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Socega itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Socega has the most support in the coalition [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 60 max = 70 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to Socega) [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 50 max = 60 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_soc_ega_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "RadSoc moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			SOC_EGA_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Socega [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 3 } }
		log = "Socega is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Socega is largest besides the ruling party [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 45 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to Socega) [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			SOC_EGA_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Socega [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 3 } }
		log = "Socega is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:social_egalitarianism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Socega is largest besides the ruling party [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to Socega) [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			SOC_EGA_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = soc_ega_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Socega [?soc_ega_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Socega is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

CEN_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-5% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-3% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "RadSoc(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+8% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+10% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-5% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-3% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
CEN_modifiers_opp_neutral = { #  this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-8% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.05 }

	else_if = {

	}
	else_if = {

		add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+8% loyalty) (cen_approval_of_government : [?cen_approval_of_government])"
	}

setup_cen_sog = {
	# Cen
	set_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 4 } }
		log = "Cen is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Cen [?cen_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Cen itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:centrism compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Cen has the most support in the coalition [?cen_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 50 max = 60 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to Cen) [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 45 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_cen_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "Cen moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			CEN_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Cen [?cen_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 4 } }
		log = "Cen is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:centrism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Cen is largest besides the ruling party [?cen_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to Cen) [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			CEN_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Cen [?cen_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 4 } }
		log = "Cen is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:centrism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Cen is largest besides the ruling party [?cen_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_social_liberalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Social Liberalism (close to Cen) [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 2 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?cen_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			CEN_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = cen_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Cen [?cen_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Cen is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-10% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.03 }

		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"

		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
TRA_CON_modifiers_opp_neutral = { #  this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-13% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-5% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "Tracon(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.07 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+7% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+8% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-13% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-5% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-2% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+8% loyalty) (tra_con_approval_of_government : [?tra_con_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
setup_tra_con_sog = {
	# Tracon
	set_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 } }
		log = "Tracon is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Tracon [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Tracon itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Tracon has the most support in the coalition [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 60 max = 70 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Fundamentalism (close to Tracon) [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 45 max = 55 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Tracon) [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_tra_con_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "Tracon moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			TRA_CON_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Tracon [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 5 } }
		log = "Tracon is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
			divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Tracon is largest besides the ruling party [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 45 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Fundamentalism (close to Tracon) [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Tracon) [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			TRA_CON_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Tracon [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 5 } }
		log = "Tracon is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Tracon is largest besides the ruling party [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Fundamentalism (close to Tracon) [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Tracon) [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			TRA_CON_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = tra_con_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Tracon [?tra_con_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Tracon is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

RAD_DEM_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-15% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-5% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "Raddem(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+8% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+10% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-5% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-5% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (rad_dem_approval_of_government : [?rad_dem_approval_of_government])"
	}
}

setup_rad_dem_sog = {
	# Raddem
	set_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 6 } }
		log = "Raddem is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Raddem [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Raddem itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:radical_democracy compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Raddem has the most support in the coalition [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 60 max = 70 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Raddem) [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_rad_dem_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "Raddem moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			RAD_DEM_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Raddem [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 6 } }
		log = "Raddem is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:radical_democracy compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 30 integer = yes }
			divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Raddem is largest besides the ruling party [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 45 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Raddem) [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } } 

				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			RAD_DEM_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Raddem [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 6 } }
		log = "Raddem is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:radical_democracy compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Raddem is largest besides the ruling party [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Raddem) [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			RAD_DEM_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = rad_dem_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Raddem [?rad_dem_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Raddem is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

CHAUV_POP_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-10% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-3% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "Chauvpop(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+8% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+10% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-10% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-3% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
CHAUV_POP_modifiers_opp_neutral = { #  this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-13% loyalty) (chauv_pop_approval_of_government : [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government])"
