setup_chauv_pop_sog = {
	# Chauvpop
	set_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 } }
		log = "Chauvpop is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Chauvpop [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Chauvpop itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:chauvinist_populism compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Chauvpop has the most support in the coalition [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 50 max = 60 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Chauvpop) [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_chauv_pop_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "Chauvpop moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			CHAUV_POP_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Chauvpop [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 7 } }
		log = "Chauvpop is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:chauvinist_populism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
			divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Chauvpop is largest besides the ruling party [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Chauvpop) [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			CHAUV_POP_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Chauvpop [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 7 } }
		log = "Chauvpop is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:chauvinist_populism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Chauvpop is largest besides the ruling party [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Chauvpop) [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			CHAUV_POP_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Chauvpop [?chauv_pop_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Chauvpop is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

AUT_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-5% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {

		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
AUT_modifiers_opp_neutral = { #  this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-13% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-5% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "Aut(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.07 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+7% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+8% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.13 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-13% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-5% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-2% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+8% loyalty) (aut_approval_of_government : [?aut_approval_of_government])"
	}
}
setup_aut_sog = {
	# Aut
	set_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 } }
		log = "Aut is in the GOVERNMENT block"
	
		if = {	# Coalition has only one member
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_gov_coalitions^num = 1 } }
			set_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Government coalition has one member: Aut [?aut_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else_if = {	# Aut itself is the largest party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 1 }
			log = "Aut has the most support in the coalition [?aut_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by ideological proximity to the coalition leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 60 max = 70 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Fundamentalism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:chauvinist_populism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 50 max = 60 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Chauvinist Populism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 45 max = 55 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Traditional Conservativism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_aut_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "Aut moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			AUT_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Aut [?aut_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 8 } }
		log = "Aut is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:autocracy compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
			divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Aut is largest besides the ruling party [?aut_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 45 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Fundamentalism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:chauvinist_populism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 45 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Chauvinist Populism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Traditional Conservativism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			AUT_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Aut [?aut_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 8 } }
		log = "Aut is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:autocracy compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 15 max = 20 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Aut is largest besides the ruling party [?aut_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Fundamentalism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:chauvinist_populism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Chauvinist Populism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Traditional Conservativism (close to Aut) [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 7 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 9 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 20 max = 25 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?aut_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			AUT_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = aut_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Aut [?aut_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Aut is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

FUN_modifiers_government = { #this block would go in scripted effects
	if = { # The value will be increased/decreased based on the political stability and the legitimacy
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.41 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 0 and 40% (-10% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.51 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 41 and 50% (-3% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.56 } }
		log = "Fun(gov): legitimacy 51-55% (no change) [?rad_soc_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.61 } }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 56 and 60% (+1% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.66 } }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 61 and 65% (+3% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 66 and 70% (+5% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { political_legitimacy < 0.71 } }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.08 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 71 and 80% (+8% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have legitimacy between 81 and 100% (+10% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}

	if = { #Political Stability
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_five = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_four = yes  PS_pol_stab_minus_three = yes } }

		subtract_from_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability between -5 and -3 (-10% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { PS_pol_stab_minus_two = yes PS_pol_stab_minus_one = yes } }
		subtract_from_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.03 }
		log = "You have political stability between -2 and -1 (-3% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_zero = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.01 }
		log = "You have political stability of 0 (-1% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_one = yes }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.02 }
		log = "You have political stability of 1 (+2% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_two = yes }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.05 }
		log = "You have political stability of 2 (+5% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { PS_pol_stab_plus_three = yes }
		add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0.10 }
		log = "You have political stability of 3 (+10% loyalty) (fun_approval_of_government : [?fun_approval_of_government])"
	}
}

		}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 65 max = 75 integer = yes }

				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 60 max = 65 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Fun) [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_fun_to_opposition = yes 
				log = "Fun moved to opposition (no close parties in coalition)"
			}
	
			FUN_modifiers_government = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final GOVERNMENT support for Fun [?fun_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 9 } }
		log = "Fun is in the NEUTRAL block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 45 integer = yes }
			divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Fun is largest besides the ruling party [?fun_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 50 max = 55 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Traditional Conservativism (close to Fun) [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 45 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Fun) [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 40 max = 50 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			FUN_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final NEUTRAL support for Fun [?fun_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 9 } }
		log = "Fun is in the OPPOSITION block"
	
		if = {	# Largest party excluding the ruling party
			limit = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology@exclude_ruling_party value = token:fundamentalism compare = equals } }
			set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 30 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
			log = "Fun is largest besides the ruling party [?fun_approval_of_government]"
		}
		else = {	# Base support by proximity to the government leader
			if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:traditional_conservatism compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 35 max = 40 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Traditional Conservativism (close to Fun) [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:autocracy compare = equals } 
					is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
				}
				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 30 max = 35 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Gov led by Authoritarianism (close to Fun) [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { 
					OR = {
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 8 }
						is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 5 }
					} 
				}

				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 25 max = 32 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "Close parties are in the government [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
			else = { 
				set_variable_to_random = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 10 max = 15 integer = yes }
				divide_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 100 }
				log = "No close ideologies in the government [?fun_approval_of_government]"
			}
	
			FUN_modifiers_opp_neutral = yes
		}
	
		clamp_variable = { var = fun_approval_of_government min = 0 max = 1 }
		log = "Final OPPOSITION support for Fun [?fun_approval_of_government]"
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0 }
		log = "Fun is banned or unorganised (Support: 0%)"
	}
}

setup_initial_party_values = { #Should be executed on startup, elections, puppeting and when a nation gains a parliament
	# RadSoc
	setup_rad_soc_sog = yes

	# SocLib
	setup_soc_lib_sog = yes

	# SocEga
	setup_soc_ega_sog = yes

	# Cen
	setup_cen_sog = yes

	# TraCon
	setup_tra_con_sog = yes

	# RadDem
	setup_rad_dem_sog = yes

	# ChauvPop
	setup_chauv_pop_sog = yes

	# Aut
	setup_aut_sog = yes

	# Fun
	setup_fun_sog = yes
}

### SOG Value Change (Based on the add_money_with_tooltip_effect scripted effect)
# How to use it:
# set_temp_variable = { sog_to_gain = 0.xx }
# add_<ideology_name>_sog_with_tooltip_effect = yes

add_rad_soc_sog_with_tooltip_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = highest_party_ideology value = token:ideology_radical_socialism compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { sog_to_gain < 0 } } #Checks if the values are positive
			if = { #To decide if the party is in the government -> decides between loyalty and support
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = LOSE_SOG_RAD_SOC_LOYALTY_TT #Gives a TT for losing sog
			}
			else = { custom_effect_tooltip = LOSE_SOG_RAD_SOC_TT }
		}
		else = {
			if = { #To decide if the party is in the government -> decides between loyalty and support
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GAIN_SOG_RAD_SOC_LOYALTY_TT #Gives a TT for gaining sog
			}
			else = { custom_effect_tooltip = GAIN_SOG_RAD_SOC_TT }
		}
		hidden_effect = { #This section will give the values specified on the temp variable to the actual variable
			set_temp_variable = { rad_soc_sog_temp = rad_soc_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
			clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
				var = rad_soc_approval_of_government
				min = 0
				max = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

add_sog_to_all_with_tooltip_effect = {
	# fixed: the whole effect was wrapped in an if with no limit, which is invalid
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { sog_to_gain < 0 } } #Checks if the values are positive
		custom_effect_tooltip = LOSE_SOG_ALL_TT
	}
	else = { 
		custom_effect_tooltip = GAIN_SOG_ALL_TT
	}
	hidden_effect = { #This section will give the values specified on the temp variable to the actual variable
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { rad_soc_sog_temp = rad_soc_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 2 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 2 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { soc_lib_sog_temp = soc_lib_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 3 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 3 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { soc_ega_sog_temp = soc_ega_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 4 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 4 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { cen_sog_temp = cen_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 5 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 5 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { tra_con_sog_temp = tra_con_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 6 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 6 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { rad_dem_sog_temp = rad_dem_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 7 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 7 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { chauv_pop_sog_temp = chauv_pop_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 8 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 8 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { aut_sog_temp = aut_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { OR = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 9 } is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 9 } } } }
			set_temp_variable = { fun_sog_temp = fun_approval_of_government } 
			add_to_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = sog_to_gain }
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = rad_soc_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = soc_lib_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = soc_ega_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = cen_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = tra_con_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = rad_dem_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = chauv_pop_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = aut_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = { #Keeps the value of the var between 0 and 1 (0 and 100)
			var = fun_approval_of_government
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
	}
}

set_all_opposition_to_neutral = {
	for_each_loop = {
	    array = parl_opp_coalitions
	    value = parl_v
	    index = parl_i
	    
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = parl_v } } }
			add_to_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = parl_v }
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			array = parl_opp_coalitions
			index = parl_i
		}
	}
}

reverse_coalitions = {
	# Extracts the two arrays into temp_arrays
	for_each_loop = {
	    array = parl_gov_coalitions
	    value = parl_v
	    index = parl_i
	    
	    add_to_temp_array = { parl_gov_temp = parl_v }
		remove_from_array = {
			array = parl_gov_coalitions
			index = parl_i
		}
	}
	for_each_loop = {
	    array = parl_opp_coalitions
	    value = parl_v
	    index = parl_i
	    
	    add_to_temp_array = { parl_opp_temp = parl_v }
		remove_from_array = {
			array = parl_opp_coalitions
			index = parl_i
		}
	}

	# Clears the original arrays
	clear_array = parl_gov_coalitions
	clear_array = parl_opp_coalitions

	# Adds the temp_arrays into the original arrays
	for_each_loop = {
		array = parl_gov_temp
	    value = parl_v
	    index = parl_i
	    
	    add_to_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = parl_v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = parl_opp_temp
	    value = parl_v
	    index = parl_i
	    
	    add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = parl_v }
	}
}

set_all_government_members_to_opposition = { # Used if you only want one opposition member to add to the coalition (in place of reverse_coalitions)
	for_each_loop = {
	    array = parl_gov_coalitions
	    value = parl_v
	    index = parl_i
	    
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = parl_v } } }
			add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = parl_v }
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			array = parl_gov_coalitions
			index = parl_i
		}
	}
}

set_rad_soc_to_neutral = {
	if = {
		limit = { ideology_radical_socialism > 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 } } }

			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
				subtract_from_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 }
			}
			else = { add_to_array = { parl_neu_coalitions =  1 } }
			setup_initial_party_values = yes
		}
	}
}

set_rad_soc_to_coalition = {
	if = {
		limit = { ideology_radical_socialism > 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = parl_gov_coalitions^num
					value = 4
					compare = less_than
				}
				NOT = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
				add_to_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
				add_to_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
				add_to_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 }
				add_to_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			else = { 
				add_to_array = { parl_gov_coalitions =  1 } 
				add_to_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			setup_initial_party_values = yes
		}
	}
}

				remove_from_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 4 }

set_rad_soc_to_opposition = {
	if = {
		limit = { ideology_radical_socialism > 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 } } }

			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
				subtract_from_variable = {
					var = coalition_seats_num
					value = radsoc_seats^num
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 }
			}
			else = { add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions =  1 } }
			setup_initial_party_values = yes
		}
	}
}

				add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions =  9 }
				remove_from_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 9 }
			}
			else = { add_to_array = { parl_opp_coalitions =  9 } }
			setup_initial_party_values = yes
		}
	}
}

set_rad_soc_to_banned = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 } } }

		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 } }
			add_to_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
			remove_from_array = { parl_neu_coalitions = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 } }
			add_to_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
			remove_from_array = { parl_gov_coalitions = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = coalition_seats_num
				value = radsoc_seats^num
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 } }
			add_to_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
			remove_from_array = { parl_opp_coalitions = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 } }
			add_to_array = { parl_ban_coalitions = 1 }
			remove_from_array = { parl_uno_coalitions = 1 }
		}

	}
}

	set_variable = { rad_soc_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { soc_lib_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { soc_ega_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { cen_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { tra_con_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { rad_dem_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { chauv_pop_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { aut_approval_of_government = 0 }
	set_variable = { fun_approval_of_government = 0 }
	clear_array = parl_voting_approval_array
}

setup_initial_coalitions = {
	clear_coalitions = yes
	parliament_init_arrays = yes
	set_temp_variable = { parl_defer_refresh = 1 }

	##### Parties with no popular support: unorganised, or banned if the ruling party outlaws them
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 1
		end = 9
		compare = less_than_or_equals
		value = parl_p

		parl_load_party_facts = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { parl_p_exists = 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { parl_target_block = 5 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { parl_p = 1 }							# RadSoc
							OR = {
								has_government = autocracy
								has_government = fundamentalism
								has_government = chauvinist_populism
								has_government = radical_democracy
							}
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { parl_p = 7 }							# ChauvPop
							has_government = ideology_radical_socialism
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { parl_p = 8 }							# Aut
							OR = {
								has_government = ideology_social_liberalism
								has_government = social_egalitarianism
								has_government = radical_democracy
							}
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { parl_p = 9 }							# Fun
							has_government = ideology_radical_socialism
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { parl_target_block = 4 }
			}
			parl_move_party = yes
		}
	}

	##### Who governs, and who sits in the neutral block. Everyone else opposes.
	set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 0 }
	clear_temp_array = parl_init_neutral

	if = {
		limit = { has_government = ideology_radical_socialism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 1 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 2 }								# SocLib
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 3 }								# SocEga
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = ideology_social_liberalism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 2 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 1 }								# RadSoc
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 3 }								# SocEga
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 4 }								# Cen
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = social_egalitarianism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 3 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 1 }								# RadSoc
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 2 }								# SocLib
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = centrism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 4 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 2 }								# SocLib
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 3 }								# SocEga
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = traditional_conservatism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 5 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = radical_democracy }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 6 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 7 }								# ChauvPop
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 8 }								# Aut
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = chauvinist_populism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 7 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 8 }								# Aut
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = autocracy }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 8 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 5 }								# TraCon
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 7 }								# ChauvPop
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 9 }								# Fun
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_government = fundamentalism }
		set_temp_variable = { parl_ruling_id = 9 }
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 5 }								# TraCon
		add_to_temp_array = { parl_init_neutral = 8 }								# Aut
	}

	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 1
		end = 9
		compare = less_than_or_equals
		value = parl_p

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = parl_ruling_id value = parl_p compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { parl_target_block = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { parl_init_neutral = parl_p } }
			set_temp_variable = { parl_target_block = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { parl_target_block = 3 }
		}
		parl_move_party = yes													# refused for parties with no support
	}

	set_temp_variable = { parl_defer_refresh = 0 }
	parliament_refresh_coalitions = yes
}


####################################################################################################
#
#   11. PUBLIC PER-PARTY NAMES
#
#
#       set_temp_variable = { parl_p = 5 }
#       set_temp_variable = { parl_target_block = 1 }
#       parl_move_party = yes
#
#   To move several parties at once, wrap the batch so the refresh only runs once:
#
#       set_temp_variable = { parl_defer_refresh = 1 }
#       set_tra_con_to_coalition = yes
#       set_fun_to_coalition = yes
#       set_temp_variable = { parl_defer_refresh = 0 }
#       parliament_refresh_coalitions = yes
#
####################################################################################################
